Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the matter structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 382. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 404 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to matter structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 382/404 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the matter structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 557. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 515 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the matter decomposition. By minimality, the element a^557 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 515-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
